## Part 0. Vol.2 안내

### 0.1 Vol.2의 역할

Vol.1이 출장 동선의 '기본 장면'을 덮는 데 집중했다면, Vol.2는 같은 출장에서 실제로 발목을 잡는 **심화 장면**과 **관계·전문 장면**을 다룬다. 여행 영역에서는 전화·채팅 고객센터, 에어비앤비 호스트, 병원 진료(환자 입장), 보험 청구, 독일 철도(DB) 실무, 기내 돌발 상황처럼 대화가 길어지고 되묻기가 잦아지는 장면을 보강했다. 학회 영역에서는 방사선방호·선량평가, 통계, 머신러닝, LLM·RAG, 시계열, 내분비 임상 등 **본인 연구를 영어로 설명하는 문장**과 리뷰어 답변서, 위원회 발언, 협약 협상, 연구비 논의, 웨비나·강의 진행 같은 **전문가 역할 수행 문장**을 대폭 추가했다. 공통 영역에는 전화 통화, 권유·초대 응답, 감정·위로, 불만 제기, 담화 표지처럼 대화를 매끄럽게 잇는 기능 표현을 모았다.

### 0.2 중복 배제 방법

'중복되지 않게'라는 요구는 빌드 단계의 자동 검증으로 보장했다. 모든 영어 문장을 유니코드 정규화(NFKC) → 소문자화 → 따옴표 통일 → 구두점 제거 → 공백 압축 순으로 정규화한 뒤, Vol.1 전체(3,092개)의 정규화 집합과 비교해 일치하는 문장을 제외했다. Vol.2 내부에서 같은 문장이 두 카테고리에 들어간 경우에도 먼저 나온 것만 남겼다. 따라서 Vol.2의 모든 문장은 Vol.1의 어떤 문장과도, 그리고 Vol.2의 다른 문장과도 정규화 기준으로 같지 않다. 다만 이 기준은 '문자열 동일성'이므로, 단어 하나만 다른 유사 문장까지 배제하지는 않는다. 그런 경우는 장면이 다르거나 기능이 다르도록 작성 단계에서 의도적으로 구분했다.

### 0.3 학습 경로 연계

Vol.2는 Vol.1을 대체하지 않고 그 위에 쌓는 구조이다. 권장 순서는 다음과 같다. 먼저 Vol.1의 Part 1과 ★ 표현으로 생존 기반을 만든다. 다음으로 Vol.2의 Part 1(공통 심화) 가운데 PHN·OFR·CMP·LNK를 익혀 대화가 길어질 때 버티는 힘을 기른다. 그 뒤 본인의 역할에 따라 Vol.2 Part 3을 선택 학습한다. 발표자라면 RPX·LLM·STX·SCI·PSQ·QA3을, 좌장·패널이라면 MOD·CMT·KEY를, 협력 담당이라면 COL(Vol.1)·DAT·MOU·GRT를 먼저 보는 것이 효율적이다. 여행 심화(Part 2)는 출발 전 해당 상황이 예상될 때 해당 카테고리만 골라 읽는 '참고서'로 쓰면 된다.

학습 방법은 Vol.1 Part 0.5의 네 단계 루틴(인지 → 대체 연습 → 인출 연습 → 간격 반복)을 그대로 적용한다. 특히 Part 3의 전문 설명 문장은 본인 연구의 실제 수치·모델명·기관명으로 바꿔 말해 보는 **개인화 대체 연습**이 효과가 크다.

## 신규 패턴 15종

Vol.1의 114개 패턴으로 포착되지 않던 기능을 보완하기 위해 추가한 패턴이다.

| ID | 패턴 | 기능 | 사용 메모 | Vol.2 수록 |
|---|---|---|---|---:|
| A15 | **Would you like ___?** | 권유·제공 | 상대에게 무엇을 권할 때. 대답은 Yes, please / No, thanks. | 14 |
| A16 | **Would it help if I ___?** | 협조적 도움 제안 | 상대 부담을 덜어주면서 내 행동을 제안한다. | 8 |
| B17 | **What kind of ___?** | 종류·유형 묻기 | 선택지를 모를 때 범주부터 묻는다. | 12 |
| B18 | **Is it worth ___?** | 가치 판단 묻기 | 현지인·전문가의 솔직한 의견을 끌어낸다. | 10 |
| C15 | **I'm calling about ___ / I'm calling to ___** | 전화·메시지 목적 | 통화 첫 문장. 용건을 먼저 말하면 상대가 빨리 연결해 준다. | 10 |
| C16 | **I'm thinking of ___ing** | 계획·고려 중 | 확정 전 의향을 부드럽게 밝힌다. | 5 |
| E14 | **I'm sorry to hear ___** | 위로·공감 | 사과가 아니라 공감. 상대 잘못이 아니어도 쓴다. | 7 |
| E15 | **Make sure ___ / Don't forget to ___** | 당부·주의 | 동료·학생에게 지시를 부드럽게 전한다. | 22 |
| F18 | **It depends on ___** | 조건부 답 | 단정을 피하면서도 정확한 조건을 제시한다. | 15 |
| F19 | **The thing is, ___** | 속사정·핵심 문제 제기 | 구어에서 본론이나 제약을 꺼낼 때 매우 자연스럽다. | 13 |
| F20 | **In terms of ___** | 관점 한정 | 비교 기준·측면을 먼저 밝히고 말한다. | 11 |
| G10 | **Let me show you how ___** | 시연 안내 | 소프트웨어·절차를 보여줄 때. | 7 |
| G11 | **If you ___, you'll see ___** | 조작·조건 안내 | 시연·튜토리얼에서 행동과 결과를 연결한다. | 9 |
| J07 | **Where are we with ___?** | 진행 상황 확인 | 프로젝트·회의에서 상태 점검. | 6 |
| K09 | **We are afraid we are unable to ___** | 서면 거절·불가 통지 | 공식 서신의 정중한 불가 표현. | 5 |

## Part 1. 공통 의사소통 심화

### `U-PHN` 전화 통화 — 예약·문의·고객센터

*상황 53개 · ★ 3개*


**A02 · Could you ___ (for me)?** — 상대에게 행동을 부탁

- ★ **Could you put me through to the front desk?** — 프런트 연결 부탁 `U-PHN-001`
- **Could you connect me to extension 214?** — 내선 연결 `U-PHN-002`
- **Could you ask her to call me back?** — 회신 부탁 `U-PHN-003`
- **Could you spell your last name for me?** — 성 철자 요청 `U-PHN-004`
- **Could you send me an email confirming that?** — 이메일 확인서 `U-PHN-005`

**A04 · I'd like (to) ___** — 원하는 것을 정중히 밝힘

- **I'd like to speak to someone in customer service.** — 고객센터 연결 `U-PHN-006`

**A06 · Would it be possible to ___? / Is it possible to ___?** — 규정 밖·예외 요청

- **Would it be possible to make an exception this time?** — 이번만 예외 요청 `U-PHN-007`

**A09 · Can I ___? / Is it okay if I ___?** — 허락 구하기

- **Can I leave a message?** — 메시지 남기기 `U-PHN-008`

**B02 · How do I get to ___? / How do I ___?** — 경로·사용법 묻기

- **How do I get through to a real person?** — 상담원 연결 방법 `U-PHN-009`

**B03 · Is there ___ (around here)?** — 존재 여부

- **Is there someone who speaks English?** — 영어 상담원 `U-PHN-010`

**B04 · Do you have ___?** — 보유·재고 여부

- **Do you have anything available this afternoon?** — 오늘 오후 가능 `U-PHN-011`

**B05 · What time ___? / When ___?** — 시각·시점

- **When would be a good time to call back?** — 다시 걸 시간 `U-PHN-012`
- **What are your opening hours today?** — 오늘 영업시간 `U-PHN-013`

**B06 · How long ___?** — 소요 시간·기간

- **How long is the wait on the line?** — 통화 대기 시간 `U-PHN-014`

**B09 · Is this ___? / Does this ___?** — 예·아니오 확인

- **Is this the Hotel Adlon?** — 맞게 걸었는지 `U-PHN-015`
- **Is this call being recorded?** — 통화 녹음 여부 `U-PHN-016`

**B10 · Do I need to ___? / Do I have to ___?** — 의무·필요 여부

- **Do you need my booking reference?** — 예약번호 필요 여부 `U-PHN-017`

**B13 · Do you know ___? / Do you happen to know ___?** — 정보 보유 여부

- **Do you know who handles group bookings?** — 단체 예약 담당자 `U-PHN-018`

**B14 · Could you tell me ___?** — 간접 의문 (정중)

- **Could you tell me the direct number for that department?** — 부서 직통번호 `U-PHN-019`

**B15 · What's the ___?** — 정보 항목 묻기

- **What's the case number for this call?** — 접수 번호 `U-PHN-020`

**B16 · What if ___? / What happens if ___?** — 조건·예외 상황

- **What if there's no one who speaks English?** — 영어 상담 불가 시 `U-PHN-021`

**C05 · I think there's a mistake ___** — 오류 지적 (완곡)

- **I think there's been a mix-up with the dates on my booking.** — 예약 날짜 혼선 `U-PHN-022`

**C14 · I was told ___** — 근거 제시

- **I was told on the phone yesterday that it was free.** — 어제 통화 근거 `U-PHN-023`

**C15 · I'm calling about ___ / I'm calling to ___** — 전화·메시지 목적

- ★ **Hi, I'm calling about my reservation for tonight.** — 전화 목적 밝히기 `U-PHN-024`
- **Hi, I'm calling to ask if anyone found a black umbrella.** — 분실물 문의 전화 `U-PHN-025`
- **I'm calling to change the time of my booking.** — 예약 시각 변경 전화 `U-PHN-026`
- **I'm calling about a charge on my bill.** — 청구 문의 전화 `U-PHN-027`
- **I'm just calling to confirm my appointment tomorrow.** — 약속 확인 전화 `U-PHN-028`
- **I'm returning your call from this morning.** — 회신 전화 `U-PHN-029`

**D03 · Just to confirm, ___? / Just to make sure ___** — 확인

- **Let me read that back to you: zero, three, zero…** — 번호 복창 `U-PHN-030`

**D05 · Could you speak more slowly / write it down?** — 전달 방식 변경 요청

- **Sorry, could you speak up a little?** — 크게 말해 달라 `U-PHN-031`
- **Could you read the number out slowly?** — 번호 천천히 `U-PHN-032`
- **Could you send that to me by email instead?** — 이메일로 대신 `U-PHN-033`

**E07 · Thanks for ___** — 감사

- **Thanks for sorting that out over the phone.** — 전화 해결 감사 `U-PHN-034`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **Hello, this is Kim speaking.** — 전화 받을 때 `U-PHN-035`
- **Sorry, who's calling, please?** — 누구냐고 물을 때 `U-PHN-036`
- **Hi, this is Kim from Korea.** — 전화 건 사람 소개 `U-PHN-037`
- **Sure, I'll hold.** — 대기 수락 `U-PHN-038`
- **Yes, I'm still here.** — 대기 후 복귀 `U-PHN-039`
- **Sorry, who am I speaking with?** — 통화 상대 확인 `U-PHN-040`
- **My number is plus 82, 10, 1234, 5678.** — 내 번호 알려주기 `U-PHN-041`
- **That's K as in kilo, I as in India, M as in Mike.** — NATO 알파벳 철자 `U-PHN-042`
- **I think I pressed the wrong option.** — 자동응답 잘못 누름 `U-PHN-043`
- **Speak to an agent.** — 음성 메뉴에서 상담원 `U-PHN-044`
- **Sorry, we got cut off earlier.** — 끊긴 뒤 재통화 `U-PHN-045`
- **The line's not great, so I may ask you to repeat things.** — 연결 상태 양해 `U-PHN-046`
- **Okay, so to sum up — you'll email me the confirmation today?** — 통화 내용 정리 `U-PHN-047`
- **Great, thanks for your help. Bye now.** — 통화 마무리 `U-PHN-048`
- **Sorry, I have to go — can I call you back in ten minutes?** — 급히 끊어야 함 `U-PHN-049`
- **I think you have the wrong number.** — 잘못 걸려온 전화 `U-PHN-050`
- **Hi, this is Kim. Please call me back when you get a chance. Thanks.** — 음성사서함 남기기 `U-PHN-051`
- **Again, that's 0151 2345 678.** — 음성메시지 번호 반복 `U-PHN-052`
- **The booking's under Kim — K, I, M.** — 예약자 이름 철자 `U-PHN-053`

### `U-OFR` 권유·초대에 응하기

*상황 39개 · ★ 3개*


**A09 · Can I ___? / Is it okay if I ___?** — 허락 구하기

- **Is it okay if I bring a colleague along?** — 동료 동반 허락 `U-OFR-001`

**A15 · Would you like ___?** — 권유·제공

- ★ **Would you like something to drink?** — 음료 권유 `U-OFR-002`
- **Would you like to sit here?** — 자리 권유 `U-OFR-003`
- **Would you like a hand with that?** — 도움 권유 `U-OFR-004`
- **Would you like to join us for dinner?** — 저녁 합류 권유 `U-OFR-005`
- **Would you like to go first?** — 순서 양보 권유 `U-OFR-006`
- **Would you like me to take your coat?** — 코트 받아주기 `U-OFR-007`
- **Would you like me to send you the photos?** — 사진 보내주기 제안 `U-OFR-008`
- **Would you like some more?** — 음식 더 권유 `U-OFR-009`
- **Would you like to try some of mine?** — 내 것 맛보기 권유 `U-OFR-010`

**A16 · Would it help if I ___?** — 협조적 도움 제안

- **Would it help if I carried one of those?** — 짐 들어주기 제안 `U-OFR-011`
- **Would it help if I sent you the slides?** — 자료 보내기 제안 `U-OFR-012`
- **Would it help if I booked the table?** — 대신 예약 제안 `U-OFR-013`

**B05 · What time ___? / When ___?** — 시각·시점

- **What time should I come?** — 방문 시각 `U-OFR-014`

**B10 · Do I need to ___? / Do I have to ___?** — 의무·필요 여부

- **Do I need to bring anything?** — 준비물 `U-OFR-015`

**B15 · What's the ___?** — 정보 항목 묻기

- **What's the address?** — 초대 장소 주소 `U-OFR-016`

**E10 · How about ___? / Shall we ___? / Why don't we ___?** — 제안

- **Unlike Seoul, most shops close on Sunday.** — 서울과 대조 `U-LNK-029`
- **I guess it's about a ten-minute walk.** — 추측 `U-LNK-030`
- **I could be wrong, but I think it's closed today.** — 틀릴 수도 `U-LNK-031`
- **Apparently, it's the oldest café in the city.** — 듣기로는 `U-LNK-032`
- **If I remember correctly, it's on the left.** — 기억하기로는 `U-LNK-033`

- **Anyway, that's enough about me.** — 내 얘기 그만 `U-LNK-035`

### `U-CLR` 명확히 설명하기·풀어 말하기

*상황 30개 · ★ 2개*


**D04 · What does ___ mean? / What do you mean by ___?** — 의미 확인

- **When you say "exposure," do you mean the total amount?** — 일상어 의미 확인 `U-CLR-001`

**D06 · So you mean ___? / Do you mean ___?** — 이해 확인 (되묻기)

- **So basically, you're saying it's a filter?** — 상대 설명 확인 `U-CLR-002`
- **So in other words, the smaller one is faster?** — 내 이해 확인 `U-CLR-003`

**F20 · In terms of ___** — 관점 한정

- **In terms of performance, the two are very similar.** — 성능 측면 `U-CLR-005`
- **In terms of time, it takes about a week.** — 시간 측면 `U-CLR-006`
- **In terms of safety, there's nothing to worry about.** — 안전 측면 `U-CLR-007`
- **In terms of accuracy, the new method is slightly better.** — 정확도 측면 `U-CLR-008`
- **In terms of usability, it still needs some work.** — 사용성 측면 `U-CLR-009`
- **In terms of regulation, nothing has changed yet.** — 규제 측면 `U-CLR-010`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **In simple terms, it measures how much radiation you receive.** — 쉽게 말하면 `U-CLR-011`
- **It's basically a device that records your dose.** — 정의하기 `U-CLR-012`
- **Think of it like a speedometer, but for radiation.** — 비유 (속도계) `U-CLR-013`
- **It's a kind of badge that workers wear on their chest.** — 대상 설명 `U-CLR-014`
- **It's used for checking whether people have been exposed.** — 용도 설명 `U-CLR-015`
- **The point is to keep doses as low as possible.** — 목적 설명 `U-CLR-016`
- **The difference is that one is personal and the other is environmental.** — 차이 설명 `U-CLR-017`
- **First you collect the data, then you clean it, and finally you analyze it.** — 과정 설명 `U-CLR-018`
- **That's because the units are different.** — 원인 설명 `U-CLR-019`
- **As a result, the numbers look higher than they are.** — 결과 설명 `U-CLR-020`
- **It's a bit like a librarian finding the right page in a huge library.** — 비유 (사서) `U-CLR-021`
- **It's about the size of a credit card.** — 크기 비유 `U-CLR-022`
- **It's roughly the same as a few chest X-rays.** — 양 비유 `U-CLR-023`
- **Let me try explaining that another way.** — 다른 방식으로 `U-CLR-024`
- **Let me draw it — it's easier.** — 그려서 설명 `U-CLR-025`
- **In a nutshell, it's a search engine for regulations.** — 한마디로 `U-CLR-026`
- **Am I making sense?** — 이해되는지 (부드럽게) `U-CLR-027`
- **Sorry, that was a bit technical.** — 너무 전문적이었다 `U-CLR-028`
- **Without the jargon, it just means the risk is very small.** — 전문용어 없이 `U-CLR-029`
- **That's about one in a million.** — 확률 쉽게 `U-CLR-030`

## Part 2. 여행 심화

### `T-BKG` 예약 변경·고객센터 (항공·숙소)

*상황 49개 · ★ 1개*


**A02 · Could you ___ (for me)?** — 상대에게 행동을 부탁

- **Could you resend the e-ticket to my email?** — 이티켓 재발송 `T-BKG-001`
- **Could you add a note that I'll be arriving late?** — 늦은 도착 메모 `T-BKG-002`
- **Could you transfer me to a live agent?** — 실시간 상담원 연결 `T-BKG-003`
- **Could you summarize the changes in one message?** — 변경 사항 요약 요청 `T-BKG-004`
- **Could you update my passport number on the booking?** — 여권번호 갱신 `T-BKG-005`

**A04 · I'd like (to) ___** — 원하는 것을 정중히 밝힘

- **I'd like to move my flight to the 22nd.** — 날짜 변경 `T-BKG-006`
- **I'd like to switch to the evening flight.** — 저녁 편으로 `T-BKG-007`
- **I'd like to correct a typo in my name on the ticket.** — 이름 오타 정정 `T-BKG-008`
- **I'd like to add a checked bag to my return flight.** — 귀국편 수하물 추가 `T-BKG-009`
- **I'd like to add a diabetic meal to my booking.** — 당뇨식 추가 `T-BKG-010`
- **I'd like to extend my hotel booking by one night.** — 숙박 연장 `T-BKG-011`
- **I'd like to move my stay one day earlier.** — 숙박 하루 당기기 `T-BKG-012`
- **I'd like to change to a room with a desk.** — 책상 있는 방으로 `T-BKG-013`

**A06 · Would it be possible to ___? / Is it possible to ___?** — 규정 밖·예외 요청

- **Would it be possible to change my seat without a fee?** — 좌석 무료 변경 `T-BKG-014`
- **Is it possible to upgrade using miles?** — 마일 업그레이드 `T-BKG-015`

**A09 · Can I ___? / Is it okay if I ___?** — 허락 구하기

- **Can I add breakfast to my booking?** — 조식 추가 `T-BKG-016`

**B03 · Is there ___ (around here)?** — 존재 여부

- **Is there a free change option?** — 무료 변경 옵션 `T-BKG-017`
- **Is there a waiting list for that flight?** — 항공편 대기 명단 `T-BKG-018`

**B05 · What time ___? / When ___?** — 시각·시점

- **When will I get the new confirmation?** — 새 확인서 시점 `T-BKG-019`
- **What's the deadline for free cancellation?** — 무료 취소 마감 `T-BKG-020`

**B06 · How long ___?** — 소요 시간·기간

- **How long does the refund usually take?** — 환불 처리 기간 `T-BKG-021`

**B07 · How much ___?** — 가격·양

- **What's the fare difference?** — 운임 차액 `T-BKG-022`
- **How much will the change cost in total?** — 변경 총비용 `T-BKG-023`
- **How many miles would that be?** — 필요 마일 `T-BKG-024`

**B08 · Which ___?** — 선택지 중 특정

- **Which airline do I check in with?** — 체크인 항공사 `T-BKG-025`

**B09 · Is this ___? / Does this ___?** — 예·아니오 확인

## Part 3. 학회·연구 활동 심화

### `C-PIT` 연구 엘리베이터 피치

*상황 34개 · ★ 2개*

- ★ **The annual limit for workers is 20 millisieverts, averaged over five years.** — 직무 선량한도 `C-RPX-017`
- **The new eye lens limit is 20 millisieverts per year.** — 수정체 한도 `C-RPX-018`
- **We try to keep doses as low as reasonably achievable.** — ALARA 원칙 `C-RPX-019`
- **Optimization is really where the work is, not the limits.** — 최적화 강조 `C-RPX-020`
- **We report the personal dose equivalent, Hp(10) and Hp(0.07).** — 개인선량당량 `C-RPX-021`
- **For the eye lens, ideally we'd use Hp(3).** — Hp(3) 이상 `C-RPX-022`
- **Effective dose isn't measurable directly — it's a protection quantity.** — 유효선량 성격 `C-RPX-023`
- **Operational quantities are what we can actually measure.** — 운용량 설명 `C-RPX-024`
- **The new operational quantities in ICRU Report 95 will change a lot.** — ICRU 95 변화 `C-RPX-025`
- **Have you looked at how ICRU 95 will affect your readings?** — ICRU 95 영향 질문 `C-RPX-026`
- **We use TLDs for official dosimetry and electronic dosimeters for daily control.** — 선량계 구분 `C-RPX-027`
- **The TLDs are read monthly, or quarterly for low-dose groups.** — 판독 주기 `C-RPX-028`
- **Doses below the recording level are entered as zero.** — 기록준위 `C-RPX-029`
- **If someone exceeds the investigation level, we look into it.** — 조사준위 `C-RPX-030`
- **We calibrate with a cesium-137 source.** — 세슘 교정 `C-RPX-031`
- **LiF has fairly good tissue equivalence.** — LiF 조직등가 `C-RPX-032`
- **Fading is a bit of an issue in hot weather.** — 페이딩 `C-RPX-033`
- **We subtract the background using control dosimeters.** — 배경 차감 `C-RPX-034`
- **Neutron dosimetry is still the hardest part.** — 중성자 난제 `C-RPX-035`
- **For beta exposure, the skin dose can be much higher.** — 베타 피부선량 `C-RPX-036`
- **For internal dose, we rely on whole-body counting.** — 내부피폭 측정 `C-RPX-037`
- **The committed effective dose is calculated over fifty years.** — 예탁유효선량 `C-RPX-038`
- **We estimate the intake from the measured activity.** — 섭취량 추정 `C-RPX-039`
- **The nuclide library has a big effect on the results.** — 핵종 라이브러리 영향 `C-RPX-040`
- **The minimum detectable activity is about 50 becquerels.** — 최소검출방사능 `C-RPX-041`
- **Most of the collective dose comes during outages.** — 계획예방정비 선량 `C-RPX-042`
- **Our collective dose has dropped by about 30 percent in ten years.** — 집단선량 감소 `C-RPX-043`
- **Steam generator work is usually the highest-dose job.** — 고선량 작업 `C-RPX-044`
- **Temporary shielding made a big difference.** — 임시 차폐 `C-RPX-045`
- **We've started using robots for some high-dose tasks.** — 로봇 작업 `C-RPX-046`
- **The idea is to predict the dose before the job starts.** — 사전 선량 예측 `C-RPX-047`
- **Better predictions mean better work planning.** — 작업 계획 개선 `C-RPX-048`
- **Our algorithm was originally designed to meet DOELAP standards.** — DOELAP 기준 `C-RPX-049`
- **We test the system against the IEC performance requirements.** — IEC 성능시험 `C-RPX-050`
- **We took part in an international intercomparison last year.** — 국제 상호비교 `C-RPX-051`
- **Our uncertainty budget is dominated by the energy response.** — 불확도 지배 요인 `C-RPX-052`
- **All doses are reported to the regulator every quarter.** — 분기 보고 `C-RPX-053`
- **There's a national dose registry for all radiation workers.** — 국가 선량등록 `C-RPX-054`
- **For comparison, a CT scan is around 7 millisieverts.** — CT 비교 `C-RPX-055`
- **Natural background is typically a few millisieverts a year.** — 자연방사선 `C-RPX-056`
- **Even this flight gave us a few tens of microsieverts.** — 비행 피폭 `C-RPX-057`
- **Radon is actually the biggest source of natural exposure.** — 라돈 `C-RPX-058`
- **Explaining small risks to the public is always tricky.** — 위험 소통 어려움 `C-RPX-059`
- **The LNT model is still the basis for regulation, even if it's debated.** — LNT 논쟁 `C-RPX-060`
- **Below a certain threshold, we only worry about stochastic effects.** — 확률적 영향 `C-RPX-061`
- **Emergency exposure situations have different reference levels.** — 비상 피폭 참조준위 `C-RPX-062`
- **Most countries have adopted ICRP 103, at least partly.** — ICRP 103 채택 `C-RPX-063`
- **Korea hasn't fully adopted the new eye lens limit yet, as far as I know.** — 국내 수정체 한도 `C-RPX-064`

- ★ **For every additional hour of work, the dose went up by about 0.02 millisieverts.** — 회귀계수 해석 `C-STX-010`
- **The odds ratio was 1.8, so the odds were almost doubled.** — 오즈비 `C-STX-011`
- **The relative risk was 1.3, with a confidence interval from 1.1 to 1.6.** — 상대위험 `C-STX-012`
- **We adjusted for age, sex, and job type.** — 공변량 보정 `C-STX-013`
- **Age is a confounder here, so we had to control for it.** — 교란변수 설명 `C-STX-014`
- **There was a significant interaction between plant and year.** — 상호작용 `C-STX-015`
- **We corrected for multiple comparisons using Bonferroni.** — 본페로니 `C-STX-016`
- **We controlled the false discovery rate at five percent.** — FDR 통제 `C-STX-017`
- **The data weren't normally distributed, so we used non-parametric tests.** — 비정규 → 비모수 `C-STX-018`
- **We used the Mann–Whitney U test to compare the groups.** — 맨-휘트니 `C-STX-019`
- **A chi-square test showed no difference in proportions.** — 카이제곱 `C-STX-020`
- **Since the same workers wore both dosimeters, we used a paired test.** — 대응표본 `C-STX-021`
- **The intraclass correlation was 0.91, which is excellent.** — ICC `C-STX-022`
- **Agreement between the two methods was good, but not perfect.** — 일치 양호 `C-STX-023`
- **The limits of agreement were minus 0.1 to plus 0.12 millisieverts.** — 일치 한계값 `C-STX-024`
- **There's a small systematic bias toward higher values.** — 체계 편향 `C-STX-025`
- **We used a weakly informative prior.** — 약정보 사전분포 `C-STX-026`
- **The posterior distribution is shown in grey.** — 사후분포 표시 `C-STX-027`
- **The 95 percent credible interval doesn't include zero.** — 신용구간 `C-STX-028`
- **All the chains converged, with R-hat below 1.01.** — MCMC 수렴 `C-STX-029`
- **We used bootstrapping to get the confidence intervals.** — 부트스트랩 구간 `C-STX-030`
- **We used a Cox model to look at time to the first event.** — 콕스 모형 `C-STX-031`
- **The hazard ratio was 0.75.** — 위험비 `C-STX-032`
- **The Kaplan–Meier curves separate after about two years.** — 카플란-마이어 `C-STX-033`
- **With this sample size, we had 80 percent power to detect a 10 percent difference.** — 검정력 80% `C-STX-034`
- **The study was underpowered for the subgroup analysis.** — 하위분석 검정력 부족 `C-STX-035`
- **We calculated the sample size before collecting the data.** — 사전 표본 계산 `C-STX-036`
- **We assumed the data were missing at random.** — MAR 가정 `C-STX-037`
- **Values below the detection limit were treated as left-censored.** — 좌측 중도절단 `C-STX-038`
- **Dose distributions are usually close to log-normal.** — 로그정규 분포 `C-STX-039`
- **We report the geometric mean because the data are skewed.** — 기하평균 보고 `C-STX-040`
- **Each plant has its own random intercept.** — 랜덤 절편 `C-STX-041`
- **The data are hierarchical — workers within teams within plants.** — 계층 구조 `C-STX-042`
- **There was overdispersion, so we used a negative binomial model.** — 과대산포 `C-STX-043`
- **There were a lot of zeros, so we tried a zero-inflated model.** — 영과잉 `C-STX-044`
- **The AIC favored the simpler model.** — AIC 비교 `C-STX-045`
- **The residuals look fine — no obvious pattern.** — 잔차 양호 `C-STX-046`
- **There's some heteroscedasticity at higher doses.** — 이분산 `C-STX-047`
- **We repeated the analysis without the outliers, and nothing changed.** — 이상치 제외 재분석 `C-STX-048`
- **Statistically significant doesn't mean it matters in practice.** — 통계적 ≠ 실무적 `C-STX-049`
- **We pre-registered the analysis plan to avoid p-hacking.** — 사전등록 `C-STX-050`
- **I'd rather focus on the effect size than the p-value.** — 효과크기 우선 `C-STX-051`
- **The interval is wide, so we can't say much yet.** — 넓은 구간 `C-STX-052`
- **This is an association — we can't claim causation.** — 연관 ≠ 인과 `C-STX-053`
- **Reverse causation is also possible here.** — 역인과 `C-STX-054`
- **There may be some selection bias, since only volunteers took part.** — 선택편향 `C-STX-055`
- **Measurement error would bias the estimates toward zero.** — 측정오차 희석 `C-STX-056`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **We trained a gradient-boosted tree model on about 200,000 records.** — 모델 개요 `C-MLX-013`
- **The main features were job type, work hours, and location.** — 주요 특징 `C-MLX-014`
- **Most of the gain came from feature engineering, not the model itself.** — 특징공학 효과 `C-MLX-015`
- **The most important feature by far was the work area.** — 최상위 특징 `C-MLX-016`
- **The SHAP plot shows that longer outages push the predictions up.** — SHAP 해석 `C-MLX-017`
- **We used Bayesian optimization for the hyperparameters.** — 베이지안 최적화 `C-MLX-018`
- **We used early stopping to avoid overfitting.** — 조기 종료 `C-MLX-019`
- **We added L2 regularization.** — L2 정규화 `C-MLX-020`
- **Dropout helped a little, but not much.** — 드롭아웃 효과 미미 `C-MLX-021`
- **The learning curves show the model stops improving after about 50,000 samples.** — 학습곡선 포화 `C-MLX-022`
- **The training error kept going down, but the validation error went up.** — 과적합 징후 `C-MLX-023`
- **The linear model was clearly underfitting.** — 과소적합 `C-MLX-024`
- **We used class weights to deal with the imbalance.** — 클래스 가중치 `C-MLX-025`
- **Oversampling with SMOTE didn't really help.** — SMOTE 무효 `C-MLX-026`
- **Accuracy is misleading here, so we used the F1 score.** — F1 선택 이유 `C-MLX-027`
- **With rare events, the precision-recall curve is more informative.** — PR 곡선 `C-MLX-028`
- **We calibrated the probabilities using isotonic regression.** — 확률 보정 `C-MLX-029`
- **We used grouped cross-validation so the same worker never appears in both sets.** — 그룹 교차검증 `C-MLX-030`
- **We trained on 2010 to 2019 and tested on 2020 onwards.** — 시간 분할 `C-MLX-031`
- **Performance dropped by about five percent on the external data.** — 외부 성능 하락 `C-MLX-032`
- **There's clearly some distribution shift between plants.** — 분포 이동 `C-MLX-033`
- **We tried simple domain adaptation by retraining the last layer.** — 마지막 층 재학습 `C-MLX-034`
- **An ensemble of three models gave the best results.** — 앙상블 `C-MLX-035`
- **In the end, we chose the simpler model because it's easier to explain.** — 설명 쉬운 모델 선택 `C-MLX-036`
- **Even a simple linear regression got us 80 percent of the way.** — 단순 기준선 위력 `C-MLX-037`
- **We used a small convolutional network on the glow curves.** — 글로우곡선 CNN `C-MLX-038`
- **We fine-tuned a pre-trained transformer.** — 사전학습 미세조정 `C-MLX-039`
- **Transfer learning helped a lot because our dataset is small.** — 전이학습 효과 `C-MLX-040`
- **We used data augmentation to make the model more robust.** — 데이터 증강 `C-MLX-041`
- **We generated synthetic data with Monte Carlo simulations.** — 합성 데이터 `C-MLX-042`
- **Some of the labels are noisy, which limits performance.** — 라벨 노이즈 `C-MLX-043`
- **For regulators, a black box isn't good enough.** — 블랙박스 불가 `C-MLX-044`
- **We used quantile regression to get prediction intervals.** — 분위회귀 구간 `C-MLX-045`
- **Conformal prediction gave us intervals with guaranteed coverage.** — 컨포멀 예측 `C-MLX-046`
- **We fixed all the random seeds so the results are reproducible.** — 시드 고정 `C-MLX-047`
- **Training took about two hours on a single GPU.** — 학습 시간 `C-MLX-048`
- **Inference takes less than a second per worker.** — 추론 속도 `C-MLX-049`
- **The model runs inside our intranet.** — 인트라넷 배포 `C-MLX-050`
- **We monitor the model monthly to catch drift.** — 월간 드리프트 감시 `C-MLX-051`
- **We plan to retrain it every year with new data.** — 연 1회 재학습 `C-MLX-052`
- **The model struggles with rare, very high doses.** — 고선량 희소 실패 `C-MLX-053`
- **We checked whether the errors were similar across job groups.** — 공정성 점검 `C-MLX-054`
- **We found a feature that was leaking information about the target.** — 특징 누수 발견 `C-MLX-055`
- **Once we removed it, accuracy dropped from 95 to 82 percent — more realistic.** — 누수 제거 후 `C-MLX-056`
- **The training code is on GitHub, but the data aren't.** — 코드만 공개 `C-MLX-057`

- **In terms of cost, local models are the clear winner.** — 비용 측면 로컬 우위 `C-LLM-013`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **We retrieve the most relevant paragraphs first, then let the model answer using only those.** — RAG 개요 `C-LLM-014`
- **An embedding turns each paragraph into a vector, so similar meanings end up close together.** — 임베딩 개념 `C-LLM-015`
- **We rank documents by cosine similarity.** — 코사인 유사도 `C-LLM-016`
- **Keyword search catches exact terms, and dense search catches meaning.** — 하이브리드 이유 `C-LLM-017`
- **Adding a cross-encoder reranker improved precision noticeably.** — 리랭커 효과 `C-LLM-018`
- **Chunks of about 500 tokens worked best for us.** — 최적 청크 `C-LLM-019`
- **We used a ten percent overlap between chunks.** — 청크 오버랩 `C-LLM-020`
- **We filter by document type before searching.** — 메타데이터 필터 `C-LLM-021`
- **nDCG rewards the system for putting the right document near the top.** — nDCG 개념 `C-LLM-022`
- **Recall at 10 tells us whether the right paragraph is in the top ten.** — Recall@10 개념 `C-LLM-023`
- **Mean reciprocal rank was 0.62.** — MRR 수치 `C-LLM-024`
- **BGE-M3 came out on top in both languages.** — BGE-M3 1위 `C-LLM-025`
- **Multilingual models did better than the Korean-only ones, surprisingly.** — 다국어 우세 `C-LLM-026`
- **Cross-lingual retrieval is still noticeably weaker.** — 교차언어 약세 `C-LLM-027`
- **All the models we tested are under one billion parameters.** — 10억 미만 `C-LLM-028`
- **We run the generator in 4-bit quantization.** — 4비트 양자화 `C-LLM-029`
- **Everything runs on-premises for security reasons.** — 온프렘 이유 `C-LLM-030`
- **It fits on a single 24-gigabyte GPU.** — 24GB GPU `C-LLM-031`
- **End-to-end latency is about three seconds.** — 응답 지연 `C-LLM-032`
- **We tell the model to say "I don't know" if the answer isn't in the context.** — 모른다고 답하게 `C-LLM-033`
- **Every answer has to cite the paragraph it comes from.** — 인용 강제 `C-LLM-034`
- **We measured hallucination by checking each claim against the source.** — 환각 측정 `C-LLM-035`
- **Faithfulness was about 90 percent.** — 충실도 `C-LLM-036`
- **LLM judges tend to be too lenient, so we checked a sample by hand.** — LLM 판정자 관대함 `C-LLM-037`
- **Agreement between the LLM judge and the experts was moderate.** — 판정자 일치도 `C-LLM-038`
- **The gold-standard answers were written by domain experts.** — 골드 답안 `C-LLM-039`
- **We had factual, numerical, and multi-hop questions.** — 질문 유형 `C-LLM-040`
- **Multi-hop questions were the hardest by far.** — 멀티홉 난도 `C-LLM-041`
- **Tables are a real problem — the text extraction often breaks them.** — 표 추출 문제 `C-LLM-042`
- **Most of our errors actually come from PDF parsing, not the model.** — PDF 파싱 오류 `C-LLM-043`
- **Some older reports are scanned, so we needed OCR.** — OCR 필요 `C-LLM-044`
- **We added a bilingual glossary to help with technical terms.** — 이중언어 용어집 `C-LLM-045`
- **Query expansion with synonyms helped for Korean.** — 동의어 확장 `C-LLM-046`
- **We chose RAG over fine-tuning because the regulations change.** — RAG 선택 이유 `C-LLM-047`
- **For fine-tuning, we'd need a few thousand high-quality pairs.** — 미세조정 데이터량 `C-LLM-048`
- **We're preparing data for supervised fine-tuning and DPO.** — SFT·DPO 준비 `C-LLM-049`
- **The model can call a dose calculator as a tool.** — 도구 호출 `C-LLM-050`
- **We keep the agent on a short leash — it can only read, not write.** — 에이전트 권한 제한 `C-LLM-051`
- **A human always reviews the answer before it's used officially.** — 사람 최종 검토 `C-LLM-052`
- **We re-index whenever a new ICRP publication comes out.** — 재색인 `C-LLM-053`
- **We'll release the benchmark so others can test their models.** — 벤치마크 공개 예정 `C-LLM-054`
- **We have to check the licenses of the ICRP texts before releasing anything.** — ICRP 라이선스 `C-LLM-055`
- **Running locally costs almost nothing once the hardware is in place.** — 로컬 운영비 `C-LLM-056`
- **Users liked that they could click through to the source.** — 출처 클릭 호평 `C-LLM-057`
- **Here, the model confused the dose limit for the public with the one for workers.** — 실패 예시 (한도 혼동) `C-LLM-058`

### `C-TSX` 시계열 예측 설명

*상황 27개 · ★ 2개*

**F13 · How did you ___?** — 방법 질문

- ★ **How did you validate the forecasts — rolling or a single split?** — 예측 검증 방식 `C-TSX-002`

**F14 · Did you consider ___? / Have you tried ___?** — 대안 제시형 질문

- **Did you include the outage schedule as a predictor?** — 정비일정 예측변수 `C-TSX-003`

**F18 · It depends on ___** — 조건부 답

- **It depends on how far ahead you need to forecast.** — 예측 지평에 달림 `C-TSX-004`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **We're forecasting monthly collective dose for each plant.** — 예측 대상 `C-TSX-005`
- **There's a strong cycle linked to the outage schedule.** — 정비 주기 계절성 `C-TSX-006`
- **There's a slow downward trend over the last decade.** — 장기 하락 추세 `C-TSX-007`
- **We differenced the series to make it stationary.** — 차분 `C-TSX-008`
- **A seasonal ARIMA model was our baseline.** — SARIMA 기준선 `C-TSX-009`
- **We also tried Prophet, but it didn't handle the outages well.** — Prophet 한계 `C-TSX-010`
- **Including the outage schedule as an external variable helped a lot.** — 외생변수 효과 `C-TSX-011`
- **An LSTM did slightly better on short horizons.** — LSTM 단기 `C-TSX-012`
- **We tested a time-series transformer, but it needed much more data.** — 시계열 트랜스포머 `C-TSX-013`
- **We're mainly interested in a three-month horizon.** — 3개월 지평 `C-TSX-014`
- **We used rolling-origin evaluation to mimic real use.** — 롤링 오리진 `C-TSX-015`
- **MAPE doesn't work well when values are close to zero.** — MAPE 한계 `C-TSX-016`
- **We used MASE so we could compare across plants.** — MASE 채택 `C-TSX-017`
- **The prediction intervals widen quickly beyond two months.** — 예측구간 확대 `C-TSX-018`
- **We also use the model to flag unusual months.** — 이상 월 탐지 `C-TSX-019`
- **There's a structural break when the new procedure was introduced.** — 구조 변화 `C-TSX-020`
- **It's very similar to forecasting blood glucose from CGM data.** — 혈당 예측 유사 `C-TSX-021`
- **For CGM data, we predict glucose 30 minutes ahead.** — CGM 30분 예측 `C-TSX-022`
- **We interpolated short gaps and dropped longer ones.** — 결측 보간 규칙 `C-TSX-023`
- **Using all plants together in one model worked better than separate models.** — 전역 모델 우세 `C-TSX-024`
- **We reconcile the plant-level forecasts with the national total.** — 계층 조정 `C-TSX-025`
- **Surprisingly, the seasonal naive forecast was hard to beat.** — 계절 나이브 위력 `C-TSX-026`
- **Planners use the forecasts to set dose targets for the year.** — 실무 활용 `C-TSX-027`

- **The Korean and English guidelines don't always agree.** — 한영 지침 불일치 `C-CLN-027`

- **Patients were randomized one to one.** — 1:1 무작위 `C-CLN-034`
- **It was open-label, so there may be some bias.** — 공개 라벨 `C-CLN-035`
- **About 12 percent dropped out.** — 탈락률 `C-CLN-036`
- **We did an intention-to-treat analysis.** — ITT 분석 `C-CLN-037`
- **How would this change clinical practice?** — 진료 변화 질문 `C-CLN-038`

- **Interestingly, a Japanese study found the opposite.** — 일본 연구 상반 `C-LIT-012`

- **I can't remember the authors, but I'll send you the link.** — 저자 기억 안 남 `C-LIT-019`

- **We followed the PRISMA guidelines.** — PRISMA `C-LIT-022`
- **In the end, 23 studies met our criteria.** — 포함 연구 수 `C-LIT-023`
- **The pooled estimate was 1.4, with high heterogeneity.** — 메타분석 이질성 `C-LIT-024`

### `C-WRT` 논문 문장 (쓰기용)

*상황 36개 · ★ 1개*


**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **Occupational radiation exposure remains a key concern in the nuclear industry.** — 서론 첫 문장 `C-WRT-001`
- **Over the past decade, large language models have shown promise in many domains.** — 서론 배경 `C-WRT-002`
- **However, little is known about their performance in radiation protection.** — 서론 공백 `C-WRT-003`
- **To date, no study has systematically compared embedding models on Korean regulatory texts.** — 서론 공백 강조 `C-WRT-004`
- **The aim of this study was to evaluate small multilingual embedding models for this task.** — 서론 목적 `C-WRT-005`
- **Our contributions are threefold.** — 서론 기여 `C-WRT-006`
- **We used dose records from all Korean nuclear power plants between 2010 and 2024.** — 방법 데이터 `C-WRT-007`
- **Workers with at least one year of monitoring were included.** — 방법 포함 기준 `C-WRT-008`
- **Records with missing job codes were excluded.** — 방법 제외 기준 `C-WRT-009`
- **All analyses were performed in Python 3.11 and R 4.4.** — 방법 소프트웨어 `C-WRT-010`
- **Differences were considered significant at p < 0.05.** — 방법 유의수준 `C-WRT-011`
- **Retrieval performance was evaluated using nDCG@10 and Recall@10.** — 방법 평가지표 `C-WRT-012`
- **A total of 1,200 queries were evaluated.** — 결과 개관 `C-WRT-013`
- **Table 2 summarizes the results for each model.** — 결과 표 언급 `C-WRT-014`
- **As shown in Figure 3, performance decreased with document length.** — 결과 그림 언급 `C-WRT-015`
- **BGE-M3 achieved the highest score in both languages.** — 결과 최고 모델 `C-WRT-016`
- **The difference between the top two models was not statistically significant.** — 결과 차이 비유의 `C-WRT-017`
- **This study shows that small models can achieve competitive performance.** — 고찰 요약 `C-WRT-018`
- **One possible reason is that multilingual training improves robustness to terminology.** — 고찰 해석 `C-WRT-019`
- **These findings are consistent with previous reports in the biomedical domain.** — 고찰 선행 비교 `C-WRT-020`
- **From a practical standpoint, these results support on-premises deployment.** — 고찰 실무 함의 `C-WRT-021`
- **This study has several limitations.** — 고찰 한계 도입 `C-WRT-022`
- **First, the queries were created by a small number of experts.** — 고찰 한계 첫째 `C-WRT-023`
- **Future work should include more languages and real user queries.** — 고찰 향후 `C-WRT-024`
- **In conclusion, small multilingual embedding models are a practical choice for radiation protection RAG systems.** — 결론 `C-WRT-025`
- **The authors thank the radiation protection staff who contributed to the dataset.** — 감사의 글 `C-WRT-026`
- **This work was supported by KHNP.** — 자금 진술 `C-WRT-027`
- **The benchmark will be made available upon publication.** — 데이터 가용성 `C-WRT-028`
- **These results suggest, but do not prove, that domain vocabulary matters.** — 헤징 (쓰기) `C-WRT-029`
- **It is likely that the observed difference reflects translation inconsistencies.** — 헤징 2 (쓰기) `C-WRT-030`
- **In contrast, the Korean-only model performed poorly on English queries.** — 연결어 대조 `C-WRT-031`
- **Moreover, the hybrid approach reduced the variance between query types.** — 연결어 추가 `C-WRT-032`
- **Consequently, we recommend a hybrid retrieval strategy.** — 연결어 결과 `C-WRT-033`
- **Although the sample was small, the trend was consistent.** — 연결어 양보 `C-WRT-034`
- **Here, "domain" refers to the subject area of the documents.** — 용어 정의 문장 `C-WRT-035`
- **Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) combines search with text generation.** — 약어 첫 등장 `C-WRT-036`

- ★ **0.5 mSv — "zero point five millisieverts."** — 밀리시버트 읽기 `C-SCI-001`
- **20 μSv — "twenty microsieverts."** — 마이크로시버트 읽기 `C-SCI-002`
- **3.7 × 10⁴ Bq — "three point seven times ten to the four becquerels."** — 베크렐 지수 읽기 `C-SCI-003`
- **10⁻³ — "ten to the minus three."** — 음의 지수 `C-SCI-004`
- **It went up by two percentage points, from 10 to 12 percent.** — 퍼센트포인트 `C-SCI-005`
- **That's a 20 percent increase, not 20 points.** — 퍼센트 vs 포인트 `C-SCI-006`
- **1/3 — "one third."** — 3분의 1 `C-SCI-007`
- **3/4 — "three quarters."** — 4분의 3 `C-SCI-008`
- **0.05 — "zero point zero five," or "point oh five."** — 소수 읽기 `C-SCI-009`
- **1,200,000 — "one point two million."** — 백만 단위 `C-SCI-010`
- **10–20 keV — "ten to twenty kilo-electron-volts."** — 범위 읽기 `C-SCI-011`
- **p < 0.001 — "p less than zero point zero zero one."** — 부등호 읽기 `C-SCI-012`
- **≥ 5 — "five or more," or "greater than or equal to five."** — 이상 읽기 `C-SCI-013`
- **± 0.2 — "plus or minus zero point two."** — 플러스마이너스 `C-SCI-014`
- **≈ 3 — "approximately three."** — 근사 기호 `C-SCI-015`
- **r² — "r squared."** — r 제곱 `C-SCI-016`
- **√n — "the square root of n."** — 제곱근 `C-SCI-017`
- **σ — "sigma," the standard deviation.** — 시그마 `C-SCI-018`
- **μ — "mu," the mean.** — 뮤 `C-SCI-019`
- **λ — "lambda," the decay constant.** — 람다 `C-SCI-020`
- **Σ — "the sum of."** — 합 기호 `C-SCI-021`
- **"The integral from zero to infinity."** — 적분 읽기 `C-SCI-022`
- **log₁₀ — "log base ten."** — 상용로그 `C-SCI-023`
- **ln x — "the natural log of x."** — 자연로그 `C-SCI-024`
- **dD/dt — "the derivative of dose with respect to time," or simply "the dose rate."** — 미분 읽기 `C-SCI-025`
- **Dose is proportional to time.** — 비례 `C-SCI-026`
- **Intensity is inversely proportional to the square of the distance.** — 역제곱 `C-SCI-027`
- **Ten times higher — an order of magnitude higher.** — 자릿수 차이 `C-SCI-028`
- **Two orders of magnitude — a factor of a hundred.** — 두 자릿수 `C-SCI-029`
- **The half-life is about 30 years.** — 반감기 `C-SCI-030`
- **662 keV — "six hundred sixty-two kilo-electron-volts."** — keV 읽기 `C-SCI-031`
- **μSv/h — "microsieverts per hour."** — 선량률 단위 `C-SCI-032`
- **Bq/cm² — "becquerels per square centimeter."** — 면적당 방사능 `C-SCI-033`
- **2026 — "twenty twenty-six."** — 연도 읽기 `C-SCI-034`
- **the 2010s — "the twenty-tens."** — 연대 읽기 `C-SCI-035`
- **14:30 — "two thirty in the afternoon."** — 24시간제 읽기 `C-SCI-036`
- **12.10.2026 — "the twelfth of October."** — 유럽식 날짜 읽기 `C-SCI-037`
- **v3.1 — "version three point one."** — 버전 읽기 `C-SCI-038`
- **568M parameters — "five hundred sixty-eight million parameters."** — 파라미터 수 `C-SCI-039`
- **8k tokens — "eight thousand tokens," or "eight K."** — 토큰 수 `C-SCI-040`
- **24 GB — "twenty-four gigabytes."** — 기가바이트 `C-SCI-041`
- **nDCG@10 — "N-D-C-G at ten."** — nDCG@10 읽기 `C-SCI-042`
- **Recall@5 — "recall at five."** — Recall@5 읽기 `C-SCI-043`
- **κ = 0.8 — "kappa equals zero point eight."** — 카파 읽기 `C-SCI-044`
- **It ranked first out of nine.** — 순위 표현 `C-SCI-045`
- **A ratio of three to one.** — 비율 3대1 `C-SCI-046`
- **The mean was 2.1, plus or minus 0.4.** — 평균±SD 읽기 `C-SCI-047`
- **Median 0.3, IQR 0.1 to 0.6.** — 중앙값·IQR 읽기 `C-SCI-048`
- **95% CI — "ninety-five percent confidence interval."** — 95% CI 읽기 `C-SCI-049`

### `C-ABS` 초록·포스터 문구 작성

*상황 24개 · ★ 1개*


**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **Retrieval-augmented generation is increasingly used to support regulatory work.** — 초록 배경 `C-ABS-001`
- **This study compares nine small embedding models on a bilingual ICRP corpus.** — 초록 목적 `C-ABS-002`
- **We built 1,200 expert-validated queries in Korean and English.** — 초록 방법 `C-ABS-003`
- **BGE-M3 with hybrid retrieval achieved the best performance in both languages.** — 초록 결과 `C-ABS-004`
- **Small multilingual models offer a practical option for on-premises deployment.** — 초록 결론 `C-ABS-005`
- **Keywords: radiation protection; retrieval; embedding; multilingual; ICRP.** — 초록 키워드 `C-ABS-006`
- **Do You Really Need a Big Model? Small Embeddings for Radiation Protection Search** — 포스터 제목 `C-ABS-007`
- **A bilingual benchmark on ICRP publications** — 포스터 부제 `C-ABS-008`
- **Key message: small, local, and good enough.** — 핵심 박스 문구 `C-ABS-009`
- **Figure 1. Retrieval performance (nDCG@10) by model and language.** — 그림 캡션 `C-ABS-010`
- **Table 1. Characteristics of the evaluated models.** — 표 캡션 `C-ABS-011`
- **Contact: scan the QR code or email the first author.** — 연락처 문구 `C-ABS-012`
- **Limitations: expert-written queries; single domain.** — 한계 문구 `C-ABS-013`
- **Next steps: real user queries and a third language.** — 다음 단계 문구 `C-ABS-014`
- **Hybrid search beat dense-only search for every model.** — 하이라이트 1 `C-ABS-015`
- **Korean queries were harder than English ones for most models.** — 하이라이트 2 `C-ABS-016`
- **All models run on a single consumer GPU.** — 하이라이트 3 `C-ABS-017`
- **Bar chart showing that the top three models are all multilingual.** — 대체 텍스트 `C-ABS-018`
- **Small Models, Big Documents** — 짧은 발표 제목 `C-ABS-019`
- **Can a Laptop Read the ICRP?** — 질문형 발표 제목 `C-ABS-020`
- **Let's cut this to 250 words — the limit is strict.** — 250단어 제한 `C-ABS-021`

- **The results sentence needs an actual number.** — 결과에 수치 필요 `C-ABS-023`
- **Use the past tense for methods and results, and the present for conclusions.** — 시제 규칙 `C-ABS-024`

### Part 3. 학회·연구 활동 심화

**PIT 연구 엘리베이터 피치** — *I build small language-model tools that help radiation protection staff find the right regulation in seconds.* · *The problem is that regulatory documents are huge and hard to search.*

**RPX 방사선방호·선량평가 전문 표현** — *How does your algorithm handle mixed photon and beta fields?* · *The annual limit for workers is 20 millisieverts, averaged over five years.*

**STX 통계 설명 심화** — *Why did you choose a t-test rather than a non-parametric test?* · *For every additional hour of work, the dose went up by about 0.02 millisieverts.*

**MLX 머신러닝·딥러닝 설명** — *How did you choose the features?* · *We trained a gradient-boosted tree model on about 200,000 records.*

**LLM LLM·RAG·임베딩 설명** — *How did you decide on the chunk size and overlap?* · *We retrieve the most relevant paragraphs first, then let the model answer using only those.*

**TSX 시계열 예측 설명** — *How did you validate the forecasts — rolling or a single split?* · *We're forecasting monthly collective dose for each plant.*

**CLN 내분비·임상 연구 대화** — *How did you define hypoglycemia in the study?* · *I'm an endocrinologist, so I see a lot of diabetes and thyroid patients.*

**DEM 소프트웨어·시뮬레이터 시연** — *Let me show you how the simulator works.* · *If you click here, you'll see the calculation step by step.*

**WSH 워크숍·튜토리얼 진행** — *Make sure you save your work every few minutes.* · *Welcome, everyone. This is a hands-on session, so please have your laptops ready.*

**PER 동료 피드백 주고받기** — *Could you take a quick look at my slides before tomorrow?* · *Overall, it's really clear and well structured.*

**GRT 연구비·과제·제안서 논의** — *Where are we with the budget section?* · *There's a new Horizon Europe call on AI in radiation protection.*

**MOU 기관 협약·협상** — *We'd like to propose an MOU between our two institutes.*

**MNT 학생·후배 지도·멘토링** — *How's the analysis going?*

**SCH 일정 조율·약속 변경·취소** — *Would Monday at 3 p.m. Berlin time work for you?* · *When would suit you best?* · *Something's come up — could we move our meeting to Friday?*

**ISS 업무 문제·실수·지연 대응** — *I'm afraid we'll need a few more days to finish the analysis.*

**MSG 축하·감사·위로·추천 메시지** — *I'm sorry to hear your proposal wasn't funded — the reviewers missed a good one.* · *Congratulations on your paper in Health Physics — well deserved!*

**CRR 커리어·조직 소개 대화** — *I've been at the institute for over 20 years.*

**VIS 그림·시각화 해석 심화** — *This scatter plot shows predicted versus measured dose.* · *The clusters show that Korean and English documents are mapped to similar regions.*

**LIT 문헌·선행연구 언급** — *Several studies have looked at this, but mostly in English.*

**WRT 논문 문장 (쓰기용)** — *Occupational radiation exposure remains a key concern in the nuclear industry.*

**SES 세션 중 청중 행동** — *Is anyone sitting here, or is it free?*

**BRK 휴식 시간·전시 부스·기업 대화** — *Hi, could you tell me a bit about this product?*

**PRJ 프로젝트 진행 관리** — *Where are we with the data cleaning?*

**TEC IT·장비·소프트웨어 문제 해결** — *My laptop isn't detecting the projector.*

**SCI 숫자·단위·수식 읽기** — *0.5 mSv — "zero point five millisieverts."*

**ABS 초록·포스터 문구 작성** — *Retrieval-augmented generation is increasingly used to support regulatory work.*

**NEG 정중한 거절·사양** — *We are afraid we are unable to accept your invitation this year.* · *Thank you for the invitation, but I won't be able to speak at the event this year.*

**ICE 네트워킹 화제 심화·대화 잇기** — *What did you make of the last session?*

**PSQ 포스터 앞 질의응답 심화** — *The data come from all of our plants over ten years.*

**QA3 의견·전망형 질문 답변** — *I think we'll see more local models, but it's hard to say how fast.*

**MOD 패널 사회·토론 진행** — *Welcome to our panel on AI in radiation protection.*

**KEY 기조·초청 강연 도입과 마무리** — *It's a great honor to be here, and I'd like to thank the organizers for the invitation.*

**IMP 즉흥 연설·건배사·감사 연설** — *If I could have everyone's attention for a moment.*

**EVL 심사·평가자 역할** — *Hi, I'm one of the judges. Could you give me a two-minute summary?*

**DAT 데이터 요청·공유 실무** — *I was wondering if you'd be willing to share your benchmark data.*

**COD 코드·재현성 대화** — *The repository is public on GitHub.*

**POL 정책·규제 논의** — *At the moment, there's no specific regulation for AI tools in dosimetry.*

**TCH 강의·교육 진행** — *Good morning, everyone. Today we'll cover the basics of external dosimetry.*

**WEB 웨비나·온라인 세미나 진행** — *Welcome, everyone, and thanks for joining today's webinar.*

## 부록 C. 중복 검증 기록과 근사 고지

### C.1 중복 검증 결과

| 항목 | 값 |
|---|---|
| Vol.2 원천 작성 문장 | 3022 |
| Vol.1과 정규화 일치로 제외 | 3 |
| Vol.2 내부 중복으로 제외 | 0 |
| **Vol.2 최종 채택** | **3019** |
| Vol.2 카테고리 / ★ | 93 / 126 |
| Vol.1 상황 수 (정규화 고유) | 3092 (3026) |
| 통합 상황 수 | 6111 |
| 통합 정규화 고유 표현 | 6045 |
| Vol.2 ∩ Vol.1 (정규화) | 0 (assert 통과) |
| Vol.2 내부 중복 (정규화) | 0 (assert 통과) |
| 전체 ID 중복 | 0 (assert 통과) |
| Vol.1 카테고리 코드 충돌 | 0 (assert 통과) |

Vol.1의 정규화 고유 수가 Vol.1 문서의 '고유 영어 표현' 수치(대소문자만 무시한 기준)와 약간 다를 수 있는 것은, 본 검증이 구두점과 따옴표까지 제거하는 더 엄격한 기준을 쓰기 때문이다.

### C.2 ID와 통합 데이터

Vol.1의 ID는 하나도 바뀌지 않았다. Vol.2는 Vol.1과 겹치지 않는 신규 카테고리 코드만 사용하므로, 통합 CSV에서 `id`는 그대로 전역 기본키가 된다. 통합 CSV에는 `volume` 열(1 또는 2)이 추가되었고, 패턴 CSV에는 `added_in`, `count_vol1`, `count_vol2` 열이 추가되어 HTML 사이트에서 권(卷)별 필터와 신규 패턴 표시를 바로 구현할 수 있다.

### C.3 근사·한계 고지

`[ADD]` 모든 표현은 모델이 생성했으며 원어민 검수를 거치지 않았다. 학회 발표·서면 답변서·추천서 문장은 실제 사용 전 원어민 동료의 확인을 권한다.

`[ADD]` Part 3의 수치(MRR 0.62, HbA1c 0.6 %p, TIR 55→68 %, kappa, 일치 한계 등), 인명(Dr. Weber, Dr. Rossi, Min-ji 등), 학술지·과제명 예시는 **문형 학습용 가상 예시**이다. 실제 연구 결과를 지칭하지 않으며, 실전에서는 본인 연구의 값으로 교체해야 한다.

`[DOC]` 규정·제도 관련 문장(직무 선량한도 5년 평균 20 mSv, 수정체 한도 20 mSv/년, ICRU 95, EU 261 보상, Deutschlandticket, 체코 비넷, 한국 플러그 Type C/F 호환, GDPR)은 일반적으로 알려진 사실을 소재로 썼다. 국내 수정체 한도 도입 현황처럼 시점에 따라 바뀌는 내용은 발언 전 최신 공식 자료로 확인해야 한다.

`[ADD]` 의료 응급(T-DEN, T-AIRQ의 의료인 자원 문장)과 환자 진료(T-DOC) 문장은 의사소통 연습용이다. 처치 지침으로 사용해서는 안 된다.

`[ADD]` 중복 판정은 문자열 정규화 기준이다. 의미는 같지만 표현이 다른 문장(예: 유사 기능의 다른 어순)은 중복으로 간주하지 않았다.


**제외된 문장 전체 목록** (원천 데이터에는 남아 있으며 빌드 단계에서 걸러짐)

| 사유 | 카테고리 | 문장 |
|---|---|---|
| Vol.1 중복 | CAS | Same here. |
| Vol.1 중복 | AIRQ | Sure, no problem. |
| Vol.1 중복 | MDA | How long will it take? |
